king grisli beard
great king land far awai the east had daughter who wa veri
beauti but proud and haughti and conceit that none the

onli made sport them

iron hand
there wa onc upon time king who had great forest near hi

she instantli snatch hi cap and want take awai from him
but held fast with both hand she again gave him hand
ducat but would not keep them and gave them the garden for
playth for hi children the third dai thing went just the
same she could not get hi cap awai from him and would not have her
monei
not long afterward the countri wa overrun war the king gather
togeth hi peopl and did not know whether not could offer ani
opposit the enemi who wa superior strength and had mighti
armi then said the garden boi grown and will the
war also onli give hors the other laugh and said seek
on for yourself when ar gone will leav on behind the
stabl for you when thei had gone forth went into the stabl and
led the hors out wa lame on foot and limp hobbleti jib
hobbleti jib nevertheless mount and rode awai the dark
forest when came the outskirt call iron hand three
time loudli that echo through the tree thereupon the wild man
appear immedi and said what you desir want strong
ste for go the war that you shall have and still more
than you ask for then the wild man went back into the forest and
wa not long befor stabl boi came out who led hors that
snort with it nostril and could hardli restrain and behind
them follow great troop warrior entir equip iron and
their sword flash the sun the youth made over hi three leg
hors the stabl boi mount the other and rode the head the
soldier when got near the battlefield great part the king
men had alreadi fallen and littl wa want make the rest give
wai then the youth gallop thither with hi iron soldier broke like
hurrican over the enemi and beat down all who oppos him thei
began flee but the youth pursu and never stop until there
wa not singl man left instead return the king howev
conduct hi troop bywai back the forest and call forth iron
hand what you desir ask the wild man take back your hors
and your troop and give three leg hors again all that
ask wa done and soon wa ride hi three leg hors when
the king return hi palac hi daughter went meet him and
wish him joi hi victori not the on who carri awai the
victori said but strang knight who came assist with
hi soldier the daughter want hear who the strang knight wa
but the king did not know and said follow the enemi and did
not see him again she inquir the garden where hi boi wa but
smile and said ha just come home hi three leg hors
and the other have been mock him and cry here come our
hobbleti jib back again thei ask too under what hedg have you
been ly sleep all the time said did the best all
and would have gone badli without and then wa still more
ridicul
the king said hi daughter will proclaim great feast that shall
last for three dai and you shall throw golden appl perhap the
unknown man will show himself when the feast wa announc the youth
went out the forest and call iron hand what you desir
ask that mai catch the king daughter golden appl
safe you had alreadi said iron hand you shall likewis
have suit red arbour for the occas and ride spirit
chestnut hors when the dai came the youth gallop the spot took
hi place amongst the knight and wa recogn on the king
daughter came forward and threw golden appl the knight but none
them caught but onli soon had gallop awai
the second dai iron hand equip him white knight and gave him
white hors again wa the onli on who caught the appl and
did not linger instant but gallop off with the king grew
angri and said that not allow must appear befor and tell
hi name gave the order that the knight who caught the appl
should awai again thei should pursu him and would not come
back willingli thei were cut him down and stab him
the third dai receiv from iron hand suit black armour and
black hors and again caught the appl but when wa ride off
with the king attend pursu him and on them got near
him that wound the youth leg with the point hi sword the

that the helmet fell from the youth head and thei could see that
had golden hair thei rode back and announc thi the king
the follow dai the king daughter ask the garden about hi
boi work the garden the queer creatur ha been the

shown children three golden appl which ha won

hi littl cap hi head but the king daughter went him and

wa handsom that all were amaz ar you the knight who came
everi dai the festiv alwai differ colour and who caught

the appl ar and took them out hi pocket and return them

who help you your victori over your enemi you can perform

requir well see said the king that ow thank

cat skin
there wa onc king whose queen had hair the purest gold and wa
beauti that her match wa not met with the whole face
the earth but thi beauti queen fell ill and when she felt that her
end drew near she call the king her and said promis that you
will never marri again unless you meet with wife who beauti
and who ha golden hair like mine then when the king hi
grief promis all she ask she shut her ey and di but the king
wa not comfort and for long time never thought take
anoth wife last howev hi wise men said thi will not
the king must marri again that mai have queen messeng were
sent far and wide seek for bride beauti the late queen
but there wa princess the world beauti and there had
been still there wa not on found who had golden hair the
messeng came home and had had all their troubl for noth
now the king had daughter who wa just beauti her mother
and had the same golden hair and when she wa grown the king look
her and saw that she wa just like thi late queen then said
hi courtier mai not marri daughter she the veri imag
dead wife unless have her shall not find ani bride upon the whole
earth and you sai there must queen when the courtier heard thi
thei were shock and said heaven forbid that father should marri
hi daughter out great sin good can come and hi daughter
wa also shock but hope the king would soon give such thought
she said him befor marri anyon must have three dress
on must gold like the sun anoth must shine silver
like the moon and third must dazzl the star besid thi
want mantl thousand differ kind fur put togeth
which everi beast the kingdom must give part hi skin and thu
she thought would think the matter more but the king made the
most skil workmen hi kingdom weav the three dress on golden
like the sun anoth silveri like the moon and third sparkl
like the star and hi hunter were told hunt out all the beast
hi kingdom and take the finest fur out their skin and thu
mantl thousand fur wa made
when all were readi the king sent them her but she got the
night when all were asleep and took three her trinket golden
ring golden necklac and golden brooch and pack the three
dress the sun the moon and the star nutshel and
wrap herself the mantl made all sort fur and besmear
her face and hand with soot then she threw herself upon heaven for
help her ne and went awai and journei the whole night till
last she came larg wood she wa veri tire she sat herself
down the hollow tree and soon fell asleep and there she slept
till wa middai
now the king whom the wood belong wa hunt hi dog
came the tree and began snuff about and run round and round and
bark look sharp said the king the huntsmen and see what sort
game li there and the huntsmen went the tree and when thei
came back again said the hollow tree there li most wonder
beast such never saw befor it skin seem thousand
kind fur but there li fast asleep see said the king
you can catch aliv and will take with the huntsmen
took and the maiden awok and wa greatli frighten and said
poor child that ha neither father nor mother left have piti
and take with you then thei said ye miss cat skin you will
for the kitchen you can sweep the ash and thing that
sort thei put her into the coach and took her home the king
palac then thei showe her littl corner under the staircas where
light dai ever peep and said cat skin you mai lie and
sleep there and she wa sent into the kitchen and made fetch wood
and water blow the fire pluck the poultri pick the herb sift the
ash and all the dirti work
thu cat skin live for long time veri sorrowfulli pretti
princess thought she what will now becom thee but happen
on dai that feast wa held the king castl she said
the cook mai littl while and see what go will
take care and stand behind the door and the cook said ye you mai
but back again half hour time rake out the ash
then she took her littl lamp and went into her cabin and took off the
fur skin and wash the soot from off her face and hand that her
beauti shone forth like the sun from behind the cloud she next open
her nutshel and brought out the dress that shone like the sun
and went the feast everyon made wai for her for nobodi knew
her and thei thought she could less than king daughter but
the king came her and held out hi hand and danc with her and
thought hi heart never saw ani on half beauti
when the danc wa end she curtsi and when the king look
round for her she wa gone on knew wither the guard that stood
the castl gate were call but thei had seen on the truth
wa that she had run into her littl cabin pull off her dress
blacken her face and hand put the fur skin cloak and wa
cat skin again when she went into the kitchen her work and began
rake the ash the cook said let that alon till the morn and
heat the king soup should like run now and give peep but
take care you don let hair fall into you will run chanc
never eat again
soon the cook went awai cat skin heat the king soup and
toast slice bread first nice ever she could and when
wa readi she went and look the cabin for her littl golden ring
and put into the dish which the soup wa when the danc wa over
the king order hi soup brought and pleas him well
that thought had never tast ani good befor the bottom
saw gold ring ly and could not make out how had got
there order the cook sent for the cook wa frighten when
heard the order and said cat skin you must have let hair fall
into the soup you will have good beat then went
befor the king and ask him who had cook the soup did
answer the cook but the king said that not true wa better
done than you could then answer tell the truth did
not cook but cat skin did then let cat skin come said the
king and when she came said her who ar you poor
child said she that ha lost both father and mother how came you
palac ask good for noth said she but
scullion girl and have boot and shoe thrown head but how
did you get the ring that wa the soup ask the king then she
would not own that she knew anyth about the ring the king sent
her awai again about her busi
after time there wa anoth feast and cat skin ask the cook let
her and see befor ye said but come again half
hour and cook the king the soup that like much then she
ran her littl cabin wash herself quickli and took her dress
out which wa silveri the moon and put and when she went
look like king daughter the king went her and rejoic
see her again and when the danc began danc with her after the
danc wa end she manag slip out slyli that the king did
not see where she wa gone but she sprang into her littl cabin and
made herself into cat skin again and went into the kitchen cook the
soup whilst the cook wa abov stair she got the golden necklac and
drop into the soup then wa brought the king who at
and pleas him well befor sent for the cook who
wa again forc tell him that cat skin had cook cat skin wa
brought again befor the king but she still told him that she wa onli
fit have boot and shoe thrown her head
but when the king had order feast got readi for the third
time happen just the same befor you must witch
cat skin said the cook for you alwai put someth into your soup
that pleas the king better than mine howev let her
befor then she put her dress which sparkl like the star and
went into the ball room and the king danc with her again and
thought she had never look beauti she did then whilst
wa danc with her put gold ring her finger without her
see and order that the danc should kept long time
when wa end would have held her fast the hand but she
slip awai and sprang quickli through the crowd that lost sight
her and she ran fast she could into her littl cabin under

half hour she had not time take off her fine dress and threw her
fur mantl over and her hast did not blacken herself all over
with soot but left on her finger white
then she ran into the kitchen and cook the king soup and soon
the cook wa gone she put the golden brooch into the dish when the
king got the bottom order cat skin call onc more and
soon saw the white finger and the ring that had put whilst
thei were danc seiz her hand and kept fast hold and
when she want loos herself and spring awai the fur cloak fell off
littl on side and the starri dress sparkl underneath
then got hold the fur and tore off and her golden hair and
beauti form were seen and she could longer hide herself she
wash the soot and ash from her face and showe herself the
most beauti princess upon the face the earth but the king said
you ar belov bride and will never more part from each
other and the wed feast wa held and merri dai wa ever
wa heard seen that countri ind ani other
snow white and rose red
there wa onc poor widow who live lone cottag front
the cottag wa garden wherein stood two rose tree on which bore
white and the other red rose she had two children who were like the
two rose tree and on wa call snow white and the other rose red
thei were good and happi busi and cheer ever two children
the world were onli snow white wa more quiet and gentl than
rose red rose red like better run about the meadowi and field
seek flower and catch butterfli but snow white sat home
with her mother and help her with her housework read her when
there wa noth
the two children were fond on anoth that thei alwai held each
other the hand when thei went out togeth and when snow white said
will not leav each other rose red answer never long
live and their mother would add what on ha she must share with the
other
thei often ran about the forest alon and gather red berri and
beast did them ani harm but came close them trustfulli the littl
share would eat cabbag leaf out their hand the roe graze
their side the stag leapt merrili them and the bird sat still upon
the bough and sang whatev thei knew
mishap overtook them thei had staye too late the forest and
night came thei laid themselv down near on anoth upon the moss
and slept until morn came and their mother knew thi and did not
worri their account
onc when thei had spent the night the wood and the dawn had rous
them thei saw beauti child shine white dress sit near
their bed got and look quit kindli them but said noth
and went into the forest and when thei look round thei found that
thei had been sleep quit close precipic and would certainli
have fallen into the dark thei had gone onli few pace
further and their mother told them that must have been the angel who
watch over good children
snow white and rose red kept their mother littl cottag neat that
wa pleasur look insid the summer rose red took care
the hous and everi morn laid wreath flower her mother
bed befor she awok which wa rose from each tree the winter
snow white lit the fire and hung the kettl the hob the kettl
wa brass and shone like gold brightli wa polish the
even when the snowflak fell the mother said snow white and
bolt the door and then thei sat round the hearth and the mother took
her spectacl and read aloud out larg book and the two girl
listen thei sat and spun and close them lai lamb upon the
floor and behind them upon perch sat white dove with it head
hidden beneath it wing
on even thei were thu sit comfort togeth someon
knock the door wish let the mother said
quick rose red open the door must travel who seek
shelter rose red went and push back the bolt think that wa
poor man but wa not wa bear that stretch hi broad black
head within the door
rose red scream and sprang back the lamb bleat the dove flutter
and snow white hid herself behind her mother bed but the bear began
speak and said not afraid will you harm
half frozen and onli want warm myself littl besid you
poor bear said the mother lie down the fire onli take care that
you not burn your coat then she cri snow white rose red come
out the bear will you harm mean well thei both came
out and and the lamb and dove came nearer and were not afraid
him the bear said here children knock the snow out coat
littl thei brought the broom and swept the bear hide clean
and stretch himself the fire and growl contentedli and
comfort wa not long befor thei grew quit home and playe
trick with their clumsi guest thei tug hi hair with their hand
put their feet upon hi back and roll him about thei took
hazel switch and beat him and when growl thei laugh but the
bear took all good part onli when thei were too rough call
out leav aliv children
snow white rose red
will you beat your wooer dead
when wa bed time and the other went bed the mother said the
bear you can lie there the hearth and then you will safe from
the cold and the bad weather soon dai dawn the two children
let him out and trot across the snow into the forest
henceforth the bear came everi even the same time laid himself
down the hearth and let the children amus themselv with him
much thei like and thei got us him that the door were
never fasten until their black friend had arriv
when spring had come and all outsid wa green the bear said on

must into the forest and guard treasur from the wick
dwarf the winter when the earth frozen hard thei ar oblig
stai below and cannot work their wai through but now when the sun
ha thawe and warm the earth thei break through and come out
pry and steal and what onc get into their hand and their cave
doe not easili see daylight again
snow white wa quit sorri hi departur and she unbolt the

and piec hi hairi coat wa torn off and seem snow white
she had seen gold shine through but she wa not sure about
the bear ran awai quickli and wa soon out sight behind the
tree
short time afterward the mother sent her children into the forest
get firewood there thei found big tree which lai fell the
ground and close the trunk someth wa jump backward and
forward the grass but thei could not make out what wa when

crevic the tree and the littl fellow wa jump about like dog
ti rope and did not know what
glare the girl with hi fieri red ey and cri why you
stand there can you not come here and help what ar you

the littl bit food that peopl get immedi burnt with

wa caught too fast will run and fetch someon said rose red you

ar alreadi two too mani for can you not think someth better
don impati said snow white will help you and she pull

the girl came just time thei held him fast and tri free hi

face wa not enough clip off the end beard now you have
cut off the best part cannot let myself seen peopl
wish you had been made run the sole off your shoe then took
out sack pearl which lai the rush and without anoth word
drag awai and disappear behind stone
happen that soon afterward the mother sent the two children the

the brother grim jaob and vilhelm were born
hinau near frankfurt the german state he throughout
their live thei remain close friend and both studi law marburg
univers jaob wa pioneer the studi german philologi
and although vilhelm work wa hamper poor health the brother
collabor the creation german dictionari not complet until
centuri after their death but thei were best and univers known
for the collect over two hundr folk tale thei made from oral
sourc and publish two volum nurseri and household tale
and although their intent wa preserv such materi
part german cultur and literari histori and their collect wa
first publish with scholarli note and illustr the tale soon
came into the possess young reader thi wa part due edger
tailor who made the first english translat select about
fifti stori with the amus some young friend princip
view thei have been essenti ingredi children read ever
sinc
